a hierarchy: rebellion crosses a limit, a tyrant or false cultic head directs it, and pride refuses command. Forced labor, obstructive difficulty, compulsion, and an imperative of obligation supply the coercive underside, while resolve and governance show the same capacity turned toward taking charge of an undertaking.","ayah_refs":["69:5","69:11","69:6","69:10","69:7","69:14","69:4"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Defiance meets coercive mastery","parent_semantic_invariant":"Exceeded limits, refused obedience, and imposed service are answered by a force that masters agents and elements.","parent_surprising_reach":"Transgression shifts between moral rebellion, physical overflow, tyrannical rule, forced labor, and the smooth elevated rock on which resistance finds no purchase.","key":"B","name":"Overflowing force as dominant punishment","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Water, wind, cry, or penalty rises beyond measure and overwhelms what lies before it.","active_motifs":"طغيان الماء وما يجري مجراه (`quranic:root_000936:B002/m01`); الطاغية عقوبة غالبة (`quranic:root_000936:B005/m01`); علو الماء والقوة الجارفة (`quranic:root_000937:B002/m01`); زيادة وعلو (`quranic:root_000537:B001/m01`); الريح والنسيم وهبوب الهواء (`quranic:root_000609:B003/m01`); الريح قوة وغلبة (`quranic:root_000609:B011/m01`); ريح صرصر باردة (`quranic:root_000857:B004/m01`); الواقعة النازلة الشديدة (`quranic:root_001675:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ط غ ي`: 69:5 (طَّاغِيَةِ), 69:11 (طَغَا); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)","synthesis":"The moral image of excess is made physical in flood, gale, and a punishment whose own force crosses the limit. Rising and dominance are one operation here: water swells, wind gains the upper hand, cold intensifies it, and the event lands as a severe visitation.","ayah_refs":["69:5","69:11","69:10","69:6","69:15"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Air carries life, cold, and summons","parent_semantic_invariant":"Moving air can animate, overpower, cool, voice, inflate, or leave a body at the moment of death.","parent_surprising_reach":"One medium joins meteorology and physiology: gale, scent, lung, panting, hiccup, roar, and the final departing breath.","key":"A","name":"Cold gale, contrary currents, and lost force","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Air moves as a severe current whose direction, temperature, and dominance determine the scene.","active_motifs":"الريح والنسيم وهبوب الهواء (`quranic:root_000609:B003/m01`); الرائحة والشم والطيب والنتن (`quranic:root_000609:B004/m01`); الريح قوة وغلبة (`quranic:root_000609:B011/m01`); صرصرة الصوت (`quranic:root_000857:B002/m01`); ريح صرصر باردة (`quranic:root_000857:B004/m01`); رياح مؤتفكات مختلفة المهاب (`quranic:root_000041:B004/m01`); ريح تقابل الدبور (`quranic:root_001198:B012/m01`); طغيان الماء وما يجري مجراه (`quranic:root_000936:B002/m01`); علو الماء والقوة الجارفة (`quranic:root_000937:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `ء ف ك`: 69:9 (مُؤْتَفِكَٰتُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ط غ ي`: 69:5 (طَّاغِيَةِ), 69:11 (طَغَا)","synthesis":"The wind is not an abstract weather label: it blows, smells, roars, chills, changes direction, meets a contrary current, and can become the very figure of superior force. Flood and gale share the operation of rising until ordinary orientation and resistance fail.","ayah_refs":["69:6","69:9","69:5","69:11"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Vertical forms are hollowed, split, and leveled","parent_semantic_invariant":"Tall bodies, palms, buildings, mountains, and sky lose their interior support and vertical distinction.","parent_surprising_reach":"Ruin is rendered through carpentry and anatomy: empty belly, uprooted trunk, loosened wall, torn skin, buried well, and sand ridge all undergo related structural failure.","key":"C","name":"Mountain mass reduced to low ground","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Raised, hard terrain is struck, spread, crowded, and brought level with the ground.","active_motifs":"السفل المقابل للسماء (`quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`); لزوم الأرض والتثاقل إليها (`quranic:root_000025:B006/m01`); الأَرْض الرعدة (`quranic:root_000025:B008/m01`); تجمع مرتفع صلب (`quranic:root_000217:B001/m01`); صلابة توقف الحفر (`quranic:root_000217:B005/m01`); دخول الجبال (`quranic:root_000217:B006/m01`); مصادفة رمل عريض (`quranic:root_000217:B011/m01`); الدق والهدم حتى التسوية (`quranic:root_000482:B001/m01`); الانبساط والانخفاض (`quranic:root_000482:B002/m01`); التزاحم والتراكم (`quranic:root_000482:B008/m01`); رملة أو حبل مرتفع يشبه العجيزة (`quranic:root_000985:B005/m01`); فرجة بين الرمال ونباتها (`quranic:root_000807:B007/m01`); أرض مرتفعة (`quranic:root_000537:B002/m01`); المكان المرتفع والسحاب الواقع (`quranic:root_001675:B016/m01`); الطغية الصفاة أو الموضع المرتفع (`quranic:root_000936:B006/m01`); الطغية نبذة من الشيء (`quranic:root_000936:B007/m01`); الطغية الصفاة الملساء (`quranic:root_000937:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً); `ع ج ز`: 69:7 (أَعْجَازُ); `ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ط غ ي`: 69:5 (طَّاغِيَةِ), 69:11 (طَغَا)","synthesis":"Earth is the lower plane against which mountain, broad sand ridge, elevated patch, lesser height, smooth high rock, and detached fragment are measured. Hardness first stops the digger; then pounding, spreading, trembling, and massing erase that resistance, leaving gaps and low continuous terrain where distinct heights had stood. The `ط غ و` rock and fragment motifs have no available surface-root anchor.","ayah_refs":["69:14","69:7","69:16","69:10","69:15","69:5","69:11"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Flood becomes conveyance and controlled water","parent_semantic_invariant":"Water moves from excess to transport, capture, storage, and measured delivery.","parent_surprising_reach":"The flood scene opens into hydraulic engineering: channels, catch basins, wells, receiving hands, cloud-reservoirs, and reflective coatings.","key":"A","name":"Torrent, vessel, and carried passengers","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"An incoming mass of water drives a vessel that bears bodies and burdens through the flow.","active_motifs":"طغيان الماء وما يجري مجراه (`quranic:root_000936:B002/m01`); علو الماء والقوة الجارفة (`quranic:root_000937:B002/m01`); الماء المعروف وأصل اسمه (`quranic:root_001458:B001/m01`); ظهور الماء ودخوله وكثرته (`quranic:root_001458:B002/m01`); جريان الشيء وانسياحه (`quranic:root_000240:B001/m01`); إقلال المحمول الظاهر (`quranic:root_000357:B001/m01`); أداة الحمل ومركوبه (`quranic:root_000357:B006/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ط غ ي`: 69:5 (طَّاغِيَةِ), 69:11 (طَغَا); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ج ر ي`: 69:11 (جَارِيَةِ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ)","synthesis":"Overflowing water supplies the moving medium. Water enters and fills, the vessel runs, and a carrier or fitted transport frame keeps passengers and burdens above it.","ayah_refs":["69:5","69:11","69:14","69:17"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Flood becomes conveyance and controlled water","parent_semantic_invariant":"Water moves from excess to transport, capture, storage, and measured delivery.","parent_surprising_reach":"The flood scene opens into hydraulic engineering: channels, catch basins, wells, receiving hands, cloud-reservoirs, and reflective coatings.","key":"B","name":"Catch basin, wellhead, and receiving vessel","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Water is arrested in a depression, drawn or poured, and handed into a durable receptacle.","active_motifs":"موضع يمسك الماء (`quranic:root_000018:B006/m01`); مجتمع الماء في هبطة أو حول حصن (`quranic:root_000281:B003/m01`); الجِيأة مجتمع الماء (`quranic:root_000282:B002/m01`); وقائع الماء ومناقعه (`quranic:root_001675:B008/m01`); خشب البئر وموضع الساقي (`quranic:root_001000:B004/m01`); ماء رَبَب كثير (`quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`); تلقي الخارج إلى اليد (`quranic:root_001198:B007/m01`); سقي على أفواه الإبل (`quranic:root_001198:B014/m01`); إيصال الماء بالسقي والصب (`quranic:root_001458:B003/m01`); ضم الشيء في وعاء (`quranic:root_001664:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `ج ي ء`: 69:9 (جَآءَ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ)","synthesis":"Pool, fortress-side cistern, pond, timbered wellhead, abundant water, receiving assistant, and mouth-level watering form a complete collection system. Pouring moves the resource into a vessel, and the vessel's defining operation is to gather and keep what would otherwise continue downstream.","ayah_refs":["69:10","69:9","69:15","69:17","69:11","69:12"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Flood becomes conveyance and controlled water","parent_semantic_invariant":"Water moves from excess to transport, capture, storage, and measured delivery.","parent_surprising_reach":"The flood scene opens into hydraulic engineering: channels, catch basins, wells, receiving hands, cloud-reservoirs, and reflective coatings.","key":"C","name":"Cloud reservoir and released rain","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Water accumulates overhead, then breaks from the cloud in repeated or forceful release.","active_motifs":"رباب السحاب (`quranic:root_000532:B008/m01`); الحَمَل في السماء والسحاب (`quranic:root_000357:B010/m01`); أفاويق السحاب دفعات الماء (`quranic:root_001188:B008/m01`); انبعاق عزالي السحاب بالمطر (`quranic:root_001688:B003/m01`); وقوع المطر ومساقطه (`quranic:root_001675:B003/m01`); الريحان النبات والورق (`quranic:root_000609:B012/m01`); تفطر الشجر وطول النبات (`quranic:root_000609:B017/m01`); علو الشيء وربو الأرض (`quranic:root_001529:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `و ه ي`: 69:16 (وَاهِيَةٌ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ)","synthesis":"Layered cloud and a dark water-bearing mass store the supply; cloud-drafts and burst rain release it. Falling water produces spring swelling, leafing, and plant height, so the same overhead reservoir that can overwhelm also renews the ground.","ayah_refs":["69:10","69:17","69:11","69:14","69:16","69:15","69:6","69:13"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Flood becomes conveyance and controlled water","parent_semantic_invariant":"Water moves from excess to transport, capture, storage, and measured delivery.","parent_surprising_reach":"The flood scene opens into hydraulic engineering: channels, catch basins, wells, receiving hands, cloud-reservoirs, and reflective coatings.","key":"D","name":"Watered sheen, plating, and reflected appearance","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A thin liquid-like surface changes how metal, face, fruit, or stone appears to an observer.","active_motifs":"كسوة المعدن بماء الذهب أو الفضة (`quranic:root_001458:B005/m01`); رونق كالماء في الوجه والكلام والثمر (`quranic:root_001458:B006/m01`); صفاء الماوية كالبلور والمرآة (`quranic:root_001458:B007/m01`); مرأى ومنظر ومرآة (`quranic:root_000531:B006/m01`); لمع البرق الخفي (`quranic:root_000428:B004/m01`); الصورة والهيئة المصورة (`quranic:root_000891:B003/m01`); العارض البادي من جهة (`quranic:root_001001:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `خ ف ي`: 69:18 (تَخْفَىٰ/خَافِيَةٌ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ)","synthesis":"Metal plating can disguise its substrate, while watery brightness gives face, speech, fruit, crystal, and mirror their sheen. Reflection, faint lightning, formed image, and a distant appearing mass make appearance a surface event whose truth depends on what lies beneath.","ayah_refs":["69:11","69:7","69:8","69:18","69:13"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: A message is sent, heard, and kept","parent_semantic_invariant":"Communication is a custody chain in which a charge is dispatched, carried, received, retained, and made recallable.","parent_surprising_reach":"The communication chain is materialized as herd after herd, flowing provision, a carried burden, an inward vessel, a legal deed, and an ear that acts as a gate of permission.","key":"A","name":"Dispatch, carrier, and delivered charge","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A sender releases a carrier who bears words or responsibility toward a receiver.","active_motifs":"الإرسال والانبعاث (`quranic:root_000563:B001/m01`); الرسول والرسالة (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`); السير السهل واللين (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); الرفق والتؤدة (`quranic:root_000563:B004/m01`); التتابع والقطع (`quranic:root_000563:B005/m01`); المراسلة والمسايرة (`quranic:root_000563:B008/m01`); تسميات مفردة مخصوصة (`quranic:root_000563:B011/m01`); الجري الوكيل والرسول (`quranic:root_000240:B003/m01`); دوام الرزق والعطاء (`quranic:root_000240:B006/m01`); مجاراة الشيء والجري معه (`quranic:root_000240:B007/m01`); إقلال المحمول الظاهر (`quranic:root_000357:B001/m01`); تحمّل الأمانة والوزر (`quranic:root_000357:B003/m01`); الإتيان بالشيء واستحضاره (`quranic:root_000281:B004/m01`); تلقي الخارج إلى اليد (`quranic:root_001198:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ); `ج ر ي`: 69:11 (جَارِيَةِ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ج ي ء`: 69:9 (جَآءَ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ)","synthesis":"Sending releases what had been held, and the messenger is the bearer of a statement. Easy gait, measured pace, successive groups, paired performance, and even a short projectile specify modes of transmission; agent, durable provision, and companioned movement keep the dispatch moving until a receiver takes it.","ayah_refs":["69:10","69:11","69:14","69:17","69:9"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: A message is sent, heard, and kept","parent_semantic_invariant":"Communication is a custody chain in which a charge is dispatched, carried, received, retained, and made recallable.","parent_surprising_reach":"The communication chain is materialized as herd after herd, flowing provision, a carried burden, an inward vessel, a legal deed, and an ear that acts as a gate of permission.","key":"C","name":"Deed, covenant, and protected remembrance","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A socially binding memory is held in a document, pledge, or protected charge.","active_motifs":"ذكر الحق صك ووثيقة حق (`quranic:root_000516:B008/m01`); الذكرى والتذكرة ما يذكّر (`quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`); حقيقة يلزم حفظها (`quranic:root_000347:B007/m01`); ربابة تجمع القداح (`quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`); ربابة عهد وميثاق (`quranic:root_000532:B011/m01`); ضمان الشيء والتكفل به (`quranic:root_001198:B008/m01`); تحمّل الأمانة والوزر (`quranic:root_000357:B003/m01`); الحمالة والكفالة (`quranic:root_000357:B004/m01`); مِلاك الأمر وعِماده (`quranic:root_001444:B005/m01`); توقيع الكتاب وأثره (`quranic:root_001675:B011/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ذ ك ر`: 69:12 (تَذْكِرَةً); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `م ل ك`: 69:17 (مَلَكُ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)","synthesis":"A deed of right, reminder, covenant, quiver-like repository, guarantee, carried trust, and written signature all preserve an obligation beyond the moment of speech. The affair has a structural mainstay, and failure is not mere forgetting but a breach in custody.","ayah_refs":["69:12","69:1","69:2","69:3","69:10","69:17","69:9","69:11","69:14","69:15"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Falsehood redirects signs and surfaces","parent_semantic_invariant":"Misrepresentation works by attributing what is not so, coating reality with a misleading surface, or turning perception away from its proper object.","parent_surprising_reach":"Falsehood reaches bodily motion and materials: milk promises continuity then stops, an animal runs then checks, cloth lies by its colors, metal is plated, and a charm turns a face.","key":"B","name":"Painted cloth, plated metal, and turning charm","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A fabricated outer appearance induces an observer or target to take one thing for another.","active_motifs":"الكذابة ثوب يكذب بحاله (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); كسوة المعدن بماء الذهب أو الفضة (`quranic:root_001458:B005/m01`); خرزة تقبل وجها إلى وجه (`quranic:root_001198:B016/m01`); رقية تمسك وتحبس (`quranic:root_000018:B004/m01`); مرأى ومنظر ومرآة (`quranic:root_000531:B006/m01`); رئي من الجن (`quranic:root_000531:B008/m01`); الصورة والهيئة المصورة (`quranic:root_000891:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ)","synthesis":"Multicolored cloth 'lies' by its state, plating presents base metal as precious, and a bead or binding charm turns attention and action. Mirror-image and a presenting spirit supply receivers for that manufactured appearance; formed shape can be mentally real while materially misleading.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:11","69:9","69:10","69:7","69:8","69:13"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Sovereignty rests on raised supports","parent_semantic_invariant":"Rule is sustained by a hierarchy of throne, upper position, structural mainstay, delegated care, and communal covenant.","parent_surprising_reach":"Royal elevation is tested through architecture and husbandry: a throne may be broken, a roof propped, a well timbered, a flock led, and a polity maintained by water and covenant.","key":"B","name":"Canopy, scaffold, and load-bearing frame","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Raised members hold shelter, crop, rider, or burden in an ordered structure.","active_motifs":"السقف والمظلّة المرفوعة (`quranic:root_001000:B002/m01`); تعريش الكرم والعنب (`quranic:root_001000:B003/m01`); خشب البئر وموضع الساقي (`quranic:root_001000:B004/m01`); عريش المرأة على البعير (`quranic:root_001000:B005/m01`); ركوب الدابة واعتلاؤها (`quranic:root_001000:B011/m01`); الثبات في البلد (`quranic:root_001000:B012/m01`); آلة قائمة وجزء قائم (`quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`); علو الموضع والجهة (`quranic:root_001188:B001/m01`); مواضع علو أو شق في الجسد والآلة (`quranic:root_001188:B013/m01`); إقلال المحمول الظاهر (`quranic:root_000357:B001/m01`); أداة الحمل ومركوبه (`quranic:root_000357:B006/m01`); التحامل والمشقة (`quranic:root_000357:B007/m01`); تجمع مرتفع صلب (`quranic:root_000217:B001/m01`); أجزاء موصولة يقابل بعضها بعضا (`quranic:root_001198:B010/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ)","synthesis":"Roof, vine-trellis, well frame, litter, mounted animal, and stable emplacement share the operation of bearing above a lower base. Standing machine parts, upper joints, transport fittings, and connected straps specify how the load is held; hardship appears when that frame must carry beyond easy capacity.","ayah_refs":["69:17","69:7","69:11","69:14","69:9"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Sovereignty rests on raised supports","parent_semantic_invariant":"Rule is sustained by a hierarchy of throne, upper position, structural mainstay, delegated care, and communal covenant.","parent_surprising_reach":"Royal elevation is tested through architecture and husbandry: a throne may be broken, a roof propped, a well timbered, a flock led, and a polity maintained by water and covenant.","key":"C","name":"Cultivation, custody, and delegated care","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A responsible agent maintains dependents, property, and resources until they reach a sound state.","active_motifs":"إصلاح وتربية وإتمام (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`); ربيب وربيبة ورابة (`quranic:root_000532:B005/m01`); لزوم وإقامة ودوام (`quranic:root_000532:B007/m01`); شاة رُبّى وحداثة (`quranic:root_000532:B009/m01`); قوة الشيء وتماسكه (`quranic:root_001444:B001/m01`); مِلاك الأمر وعِماده (`quranic:root_001444:B005/m01`); الماء مَلَك الأمر (`quranic:root_001444:B007/m01`); رعاية وحفظ وولاية (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); نيابة وقيام مقام غيره (`quranic:root_001273:B007/m01`); الحمالة والكفالة (`quranic:root_000357:B004/m01`); الحميل المحمول في النسب (`quranic:root_000357:B005/m01`); الإبقاء عفوا واستحياء (`quranic:root_000142:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `م ل ك`: 69:17 (مَلَكُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ب ق ي`: 69:8 (بَاقِيَةٍ)","synthesis":"Gradual upbringing, care for a stepchild, settled attendance, and a ewe kept near the house show rule as maintenance. Coherence, mainstay, water supply, substitution, guarantee, care for an unverified dependent, and merciful preservation all ask whether authority keeps what has been entrusted to it.","ayah_refs":["69:10","69:17","69:7","69:11","69:14","69:8"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Herd life follows generation, movement, and equipment","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal life is organized through mating, gestation, maturation, herd circulation, milking intervals, shelter, and predation.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lexical network turns abstract completion and return into a full pastoral economy, from conception and birth through age, watering, tack, shelter, and the hunter's blind.","key":"A","name":"Mating, conception, and approaching birth","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Male and female animals meet, conception becomes visible, and a carried young approaches emergence.","active_motifs":"ناقة بلغت حق الحمل والانتفاع (`quranic:root_000347:B008/m01`); تمام حال الحيوان وقوته (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`); أحق من الخيل يطابق خطوه أو يشتد بدنه (`quranic:root_000347:B014/m01`); ضراب الفحل وإنزاؤه (`quranic:root_001219:B003/m01`); ظهور حمل الناقة أو الشاة (`quranic:root_000531:B010/m01`); تحصن الفرس وصيرورته فحلا (`quranic:root_000609:B018/m01`); الحَمْل الباطن والثمر (`quranic:root_000357:B002/m01`); الحَمَل من الضأن (`quranic:root_000357:B009/m01`); ماء الفحل في الرحم (`quranic:root_001458:B004/m01`); اشتهاء الأنثى للفحل (`quranic:root_000248:B009/m01`); فرخ النعام (`quranic:root_000248:B010/m01`); الحامل تدنو ولادتها (`quranic:root_000548:B005/m01`); رفع الحمار رأسه عند الحمل (`quranic:root_001000:B009/m01`); تغذية ونشوء (`quranic:root_000537:B005/m01`); انتفاخ الشيء وامتلاؤه بالريح (`quranic:root_001529:B002/m01`); الطغيا الصغير من بقر الوحش (`quranic:root_000936:B009/m01`); تطاول الفحل على الشول (`quranic:root_000745:B003/m01`); إلقاء الثقل في المخالطة (`quranic:root_000482:B009/m01`); المواقعة في الجماع (`quranic:root_001675:B014/m01`); ترية الحيض (`quranic:root_000531:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `ر ج و`: 69:17 (أَرْجَآئِ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `س م و`: 69:16 (سَّمَآءُ); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)","synthesis":"Breeding readiness, mounting, deposited seed, visible pregnancy, inward burden, swelling, menstrual sign, and the nearness of birth form a continuous generational scene. A stallion rising over mares and bodily weight in intercourse make coupling explicit; young camel, lamb, ostrich chick, and wild-cattle calf mark successive products. The `ط غ و` calf motif has no available surface-root anchor.","ayah_refs":["69:1","69:2","69:3","69:4","69:7","69:8","69:6","69:11","69:14","69:17","69:12","69:10","69:13","69:16","69:15"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Herd life follows generation, movement, and equipment","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal life is organized through mating, gestation, maturation, herd circulation, milking intervals, shelter, and predation.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lexical network turns abstract completion and return into a full pastoral economy, from conception and birth through age, watering, tack, shelter, and the hunter's blind.","key":"C","name":"Herd return, alternating groups, and milking rhythm","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Animals move out and back in ordered groups, pause at water, and yield milk on recurring intervals.","active_motifs":"رجوع بعد انصراف وتثنية بعد بدء (`quranic:root_001058:B001/m01`); إبل تختلف ذهابا ومجيئا (`quranic:root_000859:B006/m01`); انتصاب وقيام بالبدن (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`); جمود ووقوف وكلال (`quranic:root_001273:B016/m01`); انتصاف النهار وقائم الظهيرة (`quranic:root_001273:B017/m01`); قوام في قوائم الشاة (`quranic:root_001273:B020/m01`); رجوع اللبن بين الحلبتين (`quranic:root_001188:B006/m01`); أخذ الشيء جرعات أو دفعات (`quranic:root_001188:B007/m01`); السير السهل واللين (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); التتابع والقطع (`quranic:root_000563:B005/m01`); اللبن والدر المتتابع (`quranic:root_000563:B006/m01`); أداة الحمل ومركوبه (`quranic:root_000357:B006/m01`); العدد سبعة وما يتفرع منه (`quranic:root_000669:B001/m01`); تكسّر التهالك في المشي والغنج (`quranic:root_001596:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ص ر ع`: 69:7 (صَرْعَىٰ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `س ب ع`: 69:7 (سَبْعَ); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ)","synthesis":"Return and repeated visitation become pastoral circulation: morning and evening groups cross, one herd arrives as another leaves, animals stand or tire, and the day reaches its central station. Milk returns between milkings and is taken in intervals; easy or swaying gait, successive droves, abundant yield, transport stock, and numbered watering cycles maintain the rhythm.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:6","69:7","69:17","69:10","69:11","69:14","69:5"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Herd life follows generation, movement, and equipment","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal life is organized through mating, gestation, maturation, herd circulation, milking intervals, shelter, and predation.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lexical network turns abstract completion and return into a full pastoral economy, from conception and birth through age, watering, tack, shelter, and the hunter's blind.","key":"D","name":"Tack, restraint, shelter, and mounted frame","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Equipment connects animal body to load, rider, shelter, and restraint.","active_motifs":"آلة قائمة وجزء قائم (`quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`); ركوب الدابة واعتلاؤها (`quranic:root_001000:B011/m01`); أداة الحمل ومركوبه (`quranic:root_000357:B006/m01`); التحامل والمشقة (`quranic:root_000357:B007/m01`); قبول الشيء برضا (`quranic:root_001198:B004/m01`); أجزاء موصولة يقابل بعضها بعضا (`quranic:root_001198:B010/m01`); إقبال العضو أو العلامة إلى جهة (`quranic:root_001198:B011/m01`); مقبض الشيء المأخوذ به (`quranic:root_000018:B012/m01`); فوق السهم موضع الوتر (`quranic:root_001188:B009/m01`); مواضع علو أو شق في الجسد والآلة (`quranic:root_001188:B013/m01`); جماعة النخل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_000891:B005/m01`); خرقة إنزال القدر (`quranic:root_000248:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ)","synthesis":"Standing hardware, diseased or constrained legs, livestock canopy, litter, grazing barrier, saddle or mount, load-bearing straps, udder cover, bridle parts, handle, notched fittings, and palm-shaped forelock create a detailed equipment scene. Even the heat-protective cloth belongs to this material practice of handling bodies and burdens.","ayah_refs":["69:7","69:17","69:11","69:14","69:9","69:10","69:13","69:12"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Plants are raised, selected, and made valuable","parent_semantic_invariant":"Cultivation converts water and soil into growth, then separates, selects, stores, and values the product.","parent_surprising_reach":"Palm trunks open onto seedbed, cloud, sap, sieve, jewelry, market, and reserved running strength rather than remaining a single botanical emblem.","key":"A","name":"Watered swelling and vegetative renewal","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Moisture enters soil and plant, producing swelling, leafing, fruit, and sustained green matter.","active_motifs":"الأرض اللينة المنبتة (`quranic:root_000025:B002/m01`); زيادة وعلو (`quranic:root_000537:B001/m01`); تغذية ونشوء (`quranic:root_000537:B005/m01`); الريحان النبات والورق (`quranic:root_000609:B012/m01`); تفطر الشجر وطول النبات (`quranic:root_000609:B017/m01`); ظهور الماء ودخوله وكثرته (`quranic:root_001458:B002/m01`); إيصال الماء بالسقي والصب (`quranic:root_001458:B003/m01`); رونق كالماء في الوجه والكلام والثمر (`quranic:root_001458:B006/m01`); رباب السحاب (`quranic:root_000532:B008/m01`); ربة نبات (`quranic:root_000532:B012/m01`); الحَمْل الباطن والثمر (`quranic:root_000357:B002/m01`); الحَمَل في السماء والسحاب (`quranic:root_000357:B010/m01`); علو الشيء وربو الأرض (`quranic:root_001529:B003/m01`); يبس الشجر (`quranic:root_000217:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ)","synthesis":"Soft fertile earth, rising growth, nourished young, leaves, lengthening shoots, water-filled fruit, rain cloud, persistent green plant, fruit burden, and spring swelling form one growth process. Dry wood marks its arrested state, while cloud and carried water explain the material source of renewal.","ayah_refs":["69:14","69:10","69:6","69:11","69:17","69:13"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Passage is oriented by fronts, roads, and edges","parent_semantic_invariant":"Movement depends on facing a destination, entering a route, negotiating boundaries, and finding a station or return point.","parent_surprising_reach":"The route includes mountain tracks, fatal wastes, courtyard approaches, receiving points, sky margins, body sides, and a horse that cannot run straight.","key":"B","name":"Old road, mountain track, and killing waste","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A traveler follows a known line through inhabited and dangerous terrain.","active_motifs":"قدم وطريق عود (`quranic:root_001058:B009/m01`); السير السهل واللين (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); مهواة ومفازة مهلكة (`quranic:root_001596:B006/m01`); التهلك في المفازة تحيرا (`quranic:root_001596:B010/m01`); عروض ونواح وأسماء فنية أو موضعية (`quranic:root_001001:B014/m01`); دخول الجبال (`quranic:root_000217:B006/m01`); مصادفة رمل عريض (`quranic:root_000217:B011/m01`); حقحقة تجهد الظهر في السير (`quranic:root_000347:B012/m01`); ثقل يشق النفس (`quranic:root_000807:B003/m01`); الشقة البعيدة (`quranic:root_000807:B005/m01`); مَلَك الطريق والوادي (`quranic:root_001444:B006/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ج ر ي`: 69:11 (جَارِيَةِ); `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ); `م ل ك`: 69:17 (مَلَكُ)","synthesis":"Ancient road, exposed courtyard or thoroughfare, established way, personal route, easy gait, mountain entry, broad sand barrier, distant journey, road-center, and lethal wilderness make a continuous travel scene. Back-breaking pace and hardship test the traveler; straightness is the norm, while bewildered circling is its failure.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:6","69:7","69:11","69:10","69:5","69:18","69:14","69:1","69:2","69:3","69:16","69:17"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Community is sustained by kin care and exchange","parent_semantic_invariant":"Groups endure through lineage, guardianship, gift, valuation, labor, and the circulation of benefit.","parent_surprising_reach":"The social body includes outsider, foundling, stepchild, elder, clan, guarantor, wage, tribute, market, and returning favor.","key":"A","name":"Clan, tribe, crowd, and recognized elder","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Individuals are placed inside lineage and assembled groups with visible leaders.","active_motifs":"جماعة الناس والرجال (`quranic:root_001273:B001/m01`); مقام وإقامة في موضع (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); جماعة يقبل بعضها على بعض (`quranic:root_001198:B009/m01`); ربة وجماعات كثيرة (`quranic:root_000532:B004/m01`); رَبْرَب قطيع (`quranic:root_000532:B014/m01`); ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
